3798/* NORMALIZE_BRIGHTNESS adjusts xscale, yscale and zscale so dye rgb (1,1,1) results
3799 in intensity 1. */
3800void
3801spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::normalize_brightness ()
3802{
3803 xscale = yscale = zscale = 1;
3804 struct xyz ret = dyes_rgb_to_xyz (1, 1, 1);
3805 xscale = yscale = zscale = 1 / ret.y;
3806 if (debug)
3807 {
3808 printf ("Normalizing xyz luminosity range\n");
3809 printf ("Combined xyz %f %f %f\n", ret.x, ret.y, ret.z);
3810 printf ("rscale %f gscale %f bscale %f\n", rscale, gscale, bscale);
3811 printf ("xscale %f yscale %f zscale %f\n", xscale, yscale, zscale);
3812 }
3813}
3814/* normalize_xyz_to_backlight_whitepoint adjusts xscale, yscale and zscale
3815 so dye rgb (1,1,1) results in sRGB white. */
3816void
3817spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::normalize_xyz_to_backlight_whitepoint ()
3818{
3819 xscale = yscale = zscale = 1;
3820 xyz whitepoint = whitepoint_xyz ();
3821 xyz dyewhitepoint = dyes_rgb_to_xyz (1, 1, 1);
3822 xscale = whitepoint.x / whitepoint.y / dyewhitepoint.x;
3823 yscale = 1 / dyewhitepoint.y;
3824 zscale = whitepoint.z / whitepoint.y / dyewhitepoint.z;
3825
3826 if (debug)
3827 {
3828 printf ("Normalizing dyes to backlight by scaling xyz\n"
3829 "Whitepoint xyz %f %f %f\n", whitepoint.x / whitepoint.y, whitepoint.y / whitepoint.y, whitepoint.z / whitepoint.y);
3830 printf ("Dye whitepoint xyz %f %f %f\n", dyewhitepoint.x, dyewhitepoint.y, dyewhitepoint.z);
3831 printf ("rscale %f gscale %f bscale %f\n", rscale, gscale, bscale);
3832 printf ("xscale %f yscale %f zscale %f\n", xscale, yscale, zscale);
3833 dyewhitepoint = dyes_rgb_to_xyz (1, 1, 1);
3834 printf ("Dye whitepoint xyz %f %f %f\n", dyewhitepoint.x, dyewhitepoint.y, dyewhitepoint.z);
3835 }
3836}

3838/* set_backlight sets the backlight to given illuminant IL and TEMPERATURE. */
3839void
3840spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::set_backlight (enum illuminants il, luminosity_t temperature)
3841{
3842 set_illuminant_to (backlight, il, temperature);
3843}
3844
3845/* normalize_dyes adjusts rscale, gscale and bscale so dye rgb (1,1,1) results
3846 in white in a given TEMPERATURE of daylight. */
3847void
3848spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::normalize_dyes (luminosity_t temperature)
3849{
3850 spectrum backlight_bck;
3851 memcpy (backlight_bck, backlight, sizeof (backlight));
3852 set_backlight (il_D, temperature);
3853 rscale = gscale = bscale = 1;
3854 xyz rxyz = get_xyz (red);
3855 xyz gxyz = get_xyz (green);
3856 xyz bxyz = get_xyz (blue);
3857 xyz whitepoint = whitepoint_xyz ();
3858
3859 color_matrix m (rxyz.x, gxyz.x, bxyz.x, 0,
3860 rxyz.y, gxyz.y, bxyz.y, 0,
3861 rxyz.z, gxyz.z, bxyz.z, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1);
3862 color_matrix n = m.invert ();
3863 n.apply_to_rgb (whitepoint.x, whitepoint.y, whitepoint.z, &rscale, &gscale, &bscale);
3864 if (debug)
3865 {
3866 printf ("Normalizing dyes to be neutral at %fK daylight\n"
3867 "Whitepoint xyz %f %f %f\n", temperature, whitepoint.x / whitepoint.y, whitepoint.y / whitepoint.y, whitepoint.z / whitepoint.y);
3868 printf ("Red dye xyz %f %f %f\n", rxyz.x, rxyz.y, rxyz.z);
3869 printf ("Green dye xyz %f %f %f\n", gxyz.x, gxyz.y, gxyz.z);
3870 printf ("Blue dye xyz %f %f %f\n", bxyz.x, bxyz.y, bxyz.z);
3871 printf ("rscale %f gscale %f bscale %f\n", rscale, gscale, bscale);
3872 printf ("xscale %f yscale %f zscale %f\n", xscale, yscale, zscale);
3873 xyz dyewhitepoint = dyes_rgb_to_xyz (1, 1, 1);
3874 printf ("Adjusted dye whitepoint xyz %f %f %f\n", dyewhitepoint.x, dyewhitepoint.y, dyewhitepoint.z);
3875 }
3876
3877 memcpy (backlight, backlight_bck, sizeof (backlight));
3878}
3879
3880void
3881change_concentration (spectrum s, luminosity_t concentration)
3882{
3883 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
3884 s[i] = pow (s[i], concentration);
3885}

3923
3924/* is_linear returns true if dyes_rgb_to_xyz behaves linearly. */
3925bool
3926spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::is_linear ()
3927{
3928 const int steps = 16;
3929 const luminosity_t max_error = (0.5 / 65536);
3930 color_matrix m = xyz_matrix ();
3931 for (int r = 0; r < steps; r++)
3932 for (int g = 0; g < steps; g++)
3933 for (int b = 0; b < steps; b++)
3934 {
3935 luminosity_t rr = r / (luminosity_t) steps;
3936 luminosity_t gg = g / (luminosity_t) steps;
3937 luminosity_t bb = b / (luminosity_t) steps;
3938 xyz v1 = dyes_rgb_to_xyz (rr, gg, bb), v2;
3939 m.apply_to_rgb (rr, gg, bb, &v2.x, &v2.y, &v2.z);
3940 if (fabs (v1.x - v2.x) > max_error
3941 || fabs (v1.y - v2.y) > max_error
3942 || fabs (v1.z - v2.z) > max_error)
3943 return false;
3944 }
3945 return true;
3946}

3948/* temperature_xyz returns XYZ of whitepoint for given TEMPERATURE. */
3949xyz
3950spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::temperature_xyz (luminosity_t temperature)
3951{
3952 spectrum_dyes_to_xyz dyes;
3953 dyes.set_backlight (il_D, temperature);
3954 return dyes.whitepoint_xyz ();
3955}
3956
3957/* get_xyz_old_observer computes XYZ values for given BACKLIGHT and spectrum S.
3958 This function is a replicate of get_xyz and is used for isolated dataset printing tasks.
3959 BACKLIGHT is the backlight spectrum.
3960 S is the spectrum to convert.
3961 FILENAME is optional filename for debug output. */
3962static inline xyz
3963get_xyz_old_observer (spectrum backlight, spectrum s, const char *filename = nullptr)
3964{
3965 xyz ret = { 0, 0, 0 };
3966 luminosity_t sum = 0;
3967 FILE *f = nullptr;
3968 if (filename)
3969 f = fopen(filename, "wt");
3970 /* TODO: CIE recommends going by 1nm bands and interpolate.
3971 We can implement that easily if that makes difference. */
3972 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
3973 {
3974 ret.x += (cie_cmf_x[i] * s[i] * backlight[i]);
3975 ret.y += (cie_cmf_y[i] * s[i] * backlight[i]);
3976 ret.z += (cie_cmf_z[i] * s[i] * backlight[i]);
3977 sum += cie_cmf_y[i] * backlight[i];
3978 if (f)
3979 fprintf (f, "%i %f\n", i * SPECTRUM_STEP + SPECTRUM_START, s[i] * backlight[i]);
3980 }
3981 if (f)
3982 fclose (f);
3983 luminosity_t scale = 1 / sum;
3984 ret.x *= scale;
3985 ret.y *= scale;
3986 ret.z *= scale;
3987 /* Argyll scales by backlight. */
3988 return ret;
3989}
3990
3991/* combined_xyz computes XYZ for two dyes DYE1 and DYE2 combined with BACKLIGHT.
3992 FILENAME and FILENAME2 are optional filenames for debug output. */
3993xyz
3994combined_xyz (luminosity_t *dye1, luminosity_t *dye2, luminosity_t *backlight, const char *filename = nullptr, const char *filename2 = nullptr)
3995{
3996 spectrum tmp;
3997 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
3998 {
3999 tmp [i] = (dye1 ? dye1[i] : 1) * (dye2 ? dye2[i] : 1);
4000 }
4001 if (filename)
4002 {
4003 FILE *f = fopen (filename, "wt");
4004 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, tmp);
4005 fclose (f);
4006 }
4007
4008 xyz ret = get_xyz_old_observer (backlight, tmp, filename2);
4009 return ret;
4010}

4013/* adjust_film_response_for_zeiss_contact_prime_cp2_lens adjusts film response for Zeiss lens. */
4014void
4015spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::adjust_film_response_for_zeiss_contact_prime_cp2_lens ()
4016{
4017 spectrum s;
4018 compute_spectrum (s, sizeof (zeiss_contact_prime_cp2_tramsmission) / sizeof (spectra_entry), zeiss_contact_prime_cp2_tramsmission, /* absorbance= */ false, /* norm= */ 100, /* min= */ 0, /* max= */ -1, /* clamp= */ false);
4019 for (int i = 0 ; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4020 film_response[i] *= s[i];
4021}

4050
4051/* simulated_response simulates density of recording BACKLIGHT filtered by FILTER1 and FILTER2
4052 on film with sensitivity RESPONSE.
4053 RESPONSE is the film response.
4054 BACKLIGHT is the light source spectrum.
4055 FILTER1 is the first filter spectrum.
4056 FILTER2 is the second filter spectrum. */
4057static luminosity_t
4058simulated_response (luminosity_t *backlight, luminosity_t *response, luminosity_t *filter1 = nullptr, luminosity_t *filter2 = nullptr)
4059{
4060 luminosity_t sum = 0;
4061 luminosity_t rsum = 0;
4062 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4063 {
4064 luminosity_t val = response[i];
4065 if (val < 0)
4066 if (backlight[i] < 0)
4067 printf ("Negative response %i %f\n", i, response[i]);
4068 assert (val >= 0);
4069 if (backlight)
4070 {
4071 if (backlight[i] < 0)
4072 printf ("Negative backlight %i %f\n", i, backlight[i]);
4073 assert (backlight[i]>=0);
4074 val *= backlight[i];
4075 }
4076 rsum += val;
4077 if (filter1)
4078 {
4079#if 0
4080 if (filter1[i] < 0 || filter1[i] > 1)
4081 printf ("Wrong filter1 %i %f\n", i, filter1[i]);
4082 assert (filter1[i] >= 0 && filter1[i] <= 1);
4083#endif
4084 val *= filter1[i];
4085 }
4086 if (filter2)
4087 {
4088#if 0
4089 if (filter2[i] < 0 || filter2[i] > 1)
4090 printf ("Wrong filter2 %i %f\n", i, filter2[i]);
4091 assert (filter2[i] >= 0 && filter2[i] <= 1);
4092#endif
4093 val *= filter2[i];
4094 }
4095 sum += val;
4096 }
4097 if (!rsum)
4098 return 0;
4099 return sum / rsum;
4100}
4101
4102/* adjust_concentration_to_y increases or decreases concentration of filter S to reach luminosity Y.
4103 BACKLIGHT is the light source spectrum.
4104 S is the filter spectrum.
4105 Y is the target luminosity.
4106 NORM is the base for no filter. */
4107luminosity_t
4108adjust_concentration_to_y (spectrum backlight, spectrum s, luminosity_t y, luminosity_t norm = 1)
4109{
4110 luminosity_t bestc = 0, bestc_y = 1;
4111 luminosity_t sum = 0;
4112 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4113 sum += cie_cmf_y[i] * backlight[i];
4114 for (luminosity_t c = 0.001 ; c < 10; c+=0.001)
4115 {
4116 luminosity_t ysum = 0;
4117 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4118 ysum += (cie_cmf_y[i] * my_pow (s[i] / norm, c) * norm * backlight[i]) ;
4119 ysum /= sum;
4120 //printf ("%f %f %f\n",y, ysum, bestc_y);
4121 if (fabs (ysum - y) < fabs (bestc_y - y))
4122 {
4123 bestc_y = ysum;
4124 bestc = c;
4125 }
4126 }
4127 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4128 s[i] = my_pow (s[i]/norm, bestc) * norm;
4129 return bestc;
4130}
4131
4132/* determine_patch_weights_by_simulated_response determines patch weights by simulated response.
4133 OBSERVER is the observer to use. */
4134rgbdata
4135spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::determine_patch_weights_by_simulated_response (int observer)
4136{
4137 xyz half_whitepoint = whitepoint_xyz (observer) * 0.5;
4138 rgbdata white = xyz_to_dyes_rgb (half_whitepoint, observer);
4139 rgbdata res = film_rgb_response (nullptr) * 0.5;
4140#if 0
4141 if (red_characteristic_curve)
4142 return {red_characteristic_curve->unapply (white.red) / red_characteristic_curve->unapply (res.red),
4143 green_characteristic_curve->unapply (white.green) / green_characteristic_curve->unapply (res.green),
4144 blue_characteristic_curve->unapply (white.blue) / blue_characteristic_curve->unapply (res.blue)};
4145#endif
4146 return {white.red / res.red, white.green / res.green, white.blue / res.blue};
4147}
4148
4149/* generate_simulated_argyll_ti3_file generates Argyll .ti3 file.
4150 F is the FILE pointer to write to. */
4151bool
4152spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::generate_simulated_argyll_ti3_file (FILE *f)
4153{
4154 adjust_exposure ();
4155 rgbdata res = film_rgb_response (nullptr);
4156 luminosity_t sum = res.red + res.green + res.blue;
4157 rgbdata scale = {1/sum, 1/sum, 1/sum};
4158 int nsamples = sizeof (TLCI_2012_TCS) / sizeof (xspect);
4159 fprintf (f, "CTI3\n\n");
4160 fprintf (f, "DESCRIPTOR \"Colorscreen produced Argyll Calibration Target chart information 3\"\n");
4161 fprintf (f, "ORIGINATOR \"Argyll target\"\n");
4162 fprintf (f, "DEVICE_CLASS \"INPUT\"\n");
4163 fprintf (f, "COLOR_REP \"XYZ_RGB\"\n\n");
4164 fprintf (f, "NUMBER_OF_FIELDS 10\n");
4165 fprintf (f, "BEGIN_DATA_FORMAT\n");
4166 fprintf (f, "SAMPLE_ID XYZ_X XYZ_Y XYZ_Z RGB_R RGB_G RGB_B STDEV_R STDEV_G STDEV_B\n");
4167 fprintf (f, "END_DATA_FORMAT\n\n");
4168 fprintf (f, "NUMBER_OF_SETS %i\n", nsamples);
4169 fprintf (f, "BEGIN_DATA\n");
4170
4171 for (int y = 0; y < (nsamples + 5) / 6; y++)
4172 {
4173 for (int x = 0; x < 6; x++)
4174 {
4175 if (y * 6 + x >= nsamples)
4176 continue;
4177 xspect &xtile = TLCI_2012_TCS [y * 6 + x];
4178 spectrum tile;
4179 compute_spectrum (tile, xtile);
4180 xyz real_color = get_xyz_old_observer (backlight, tile);
4181 rgbdata color = film_rgb_response (tile) * scale;
4182 fprintf (f, "A%c%i %f %f %f %f %f %f %f %f %f\n",
4183 'A'+y, x+1,
4184 real_color.x * 100, real_color.y * 100, real_color.z * 100,
4185 color.red * 100, color.green * 100, color.blue * 100, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
4186 }
4187 }
4188 fprintf (f, "END_DATA\n");
4189 return true;
4190}
4191/* linear_film_rgb_response returns the linear RGB response of the film.
4192 S is the spectrum of the light source. */
4193rgbdata
4194spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::linear_film_rgb_response (luminosity_t *s)
4195{
4196 return {simulated_response (backlight, film_response, red, s),
4197 simulated_response (backlight, film_response, green, s),
4198 simulated_response (backlight, film_response, blue, s)};
4199}
4200
4201/* film_rgb_response returns the RGB response of the film.
4202 S is the spectrum of the light source. */
4203rgbdata
4204spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::film_rgb_response (luminosity_t *s)
4205{
4206 rgbdata ret = linear_film_rgb_response (s);
4207 if (red_characteristic_curve)
4208 {
4209 ret.red = red_characteristic_curve->apply (ret.red * exp_adjust.red);
4210 ret.green = green_characteristic_curve->apply (ret.green * exp_adjust.green);
4211 ret.blue = blue_characteristic_curve->apply (ret.blue * exp_adjust.blue);
4212 }
4213 return ret;
4214}
4215
4216/* generate_color_target_tiff generates color target TIFF file.
4217 FILENAME is the name of the file to write.
4218 ERROR is the error message if the operation fails.
4219 WHITE_BALANCE if true, perform white balance.
4220 OPTIMIZED if true, use optimized color matrix. */
4221bool
4222spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::generate_color_target_tiff (const char *filename, const char **error, bool white_balance, bool optimized)
4223{
4224 xyz whitepoint = whitepoint_xyz ();
4225 rgbdata scale = {0,0,0};
4226 int nsamples = sizeof (TLCI_2012_TCS) / sizeof (xspect);
4227 int n = 0;
4228 adjust_exposure ();
4229 spectrum tile;
4230 if (optimized)
4231 {
4232 rgbdata film_white = film_rgb_response (nullptr);
4233 luminosity_t sum = (film_white.red + film_white.green + film_white.blue) / 3;
4234 scale.red = 1/sum;
4235 scale.green = 1/sum;
4236 scale.blue = 1/sum;
4237 }
4238 else if (white_balance)
4239 {
4240 scale = determine_patch_weights_by_simulated_response ();
4241 printf ("white balance %f%% %f%% %f%%\n",100 * scale.red, 100 * scale.green, 100 * scale.blue);
4242 }
4243 else
4244 {
4245 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4246 tile[i] = 0.5;
4247 rgbdata film_white = film_rgb_response (tile);
4248
4249 luminosity_t sum;
4250 /* For subtractive processes just be sure that backlight is 100% white on film. */
4251 if (subtractive)
4252 sum = (film_white.red + film_white.green + film_white.blue) / 3;
4253 /* For additive processes make backlight photo to have 100% white in replay.
4254 This also compensates the screen darkness. */
4255 else
4256 {
4257 xyz c = dyes_rgb_to_xyz (film_white.red, film_white.green, film_white.blue);
4258 sum = c.y;
4259 }
4260 printf ("Film response to backlight %f %f %f sum:%f\n", film_white.red, film_white.green, film_white.blue, sum);
4261 scale.red = scale.green = scale.blue = 0.5 / sum;
4262 /*determine_relative_patch_sizes_by_whitepoint ();*/
4263 /*xyz_to_dyes_rgb (whitepoint);*/
4264
4265 //scale /= sum * 0.3;
4266 }
4267 luminosity_t sum = scale.red + scale.green + scale.blue;
4268 luminosity_t sum2 = scale.red * rscale + scale.green * gscale + scale.blue * bscale;
4269 printf ("RGB scales %f %f%% %f %f%% %f %f%%\n", scale.red, 100 * scale.red / sum, scale.green, 100 * scale.green / sum, scale.blue, 100 * scale.blue / sum);
4270 printf ("RGB scales adjusted by patch sizes %f %f%% %f %f%% %f %f%%\n", scale.red * rscale, 100 * scale.red * rscale / sum2, scale.green * gscale, 100 * scale.green * gscale / sum2, scale.blue * bscale, 100 * scale.blue * bscale / sum2);
4271 color_matrix id;
4272 color_matrix m = optimized ? optimized_xyz_matrix () : subtractive ? id : xyz_matrix ();
4273 luminosity_t deltaEsum = 0;
4274 luminosity_t deltaEmax = 0;
4275
4276 void *buffer;
4277 size_t len = create_pro_photo_rgb_profile (&buffer, whitepoint);
4278 tiff_writer_params par;
4279 par.filename = filename;
4280 par.width = 12;
4281 par.height = ((nsamples + 5) / 6) * 2;
4282 par.depth = 32;
4283 par.hdr = true;
4284 par.icc_profile = buffer;
4285 par.icc_profile_len = len;
4286 tiff_writer tiff (par, error);
4287 if (*error)
4288 {
4289 free (buffer);
4290 return false;
4291 }
4292 for (int y = 0; y < (nsamples + 5) / 6; y++)
4293 {
4294 for (int d = 0; d < 2; d++)
4295 {
4296 for (int x = 0; x < 6; x++)
4297 {
4298 xspect &xtile = TLCI_2012_TCS [std::min (y * 6 + x, nsamples - 1)];
4299 compute_spectrum (tile, xtile);
4300 xyz real_color = get_xyz_old_observer (backlight, tile);
4301 rgbdata color = film_rgb_response (tile) * scale;
4302 xyz dufay_color (0,0,0);
4303 if (!subtractive || optimized)
4304 m.apply_to_rgb (color.red, color.green, color.blue, &dufay_color.x, &dufay_color.y, &dufay_color.z);
4305 else
4306 dufay_color = dyes_rgb_to_xyz (color.red, color.green, color.blue);
4307 //xyz dufay_color = dyes_rgb_to_xyz (color.red, color.green, color.blue);
4308 luminosity_t r,g,b;
4309 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (real_color.x, real_color.y, real_color.z, &r, &g, &b);
4310 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (x * 2, r, g, b);
4311 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (dufay_color.x, dufay_color.y, dufay_color.z, &r, &g, &b);
4312 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (x * 2 + 1, r, g, b);
4313 if (y * 6 + x < nsamples)
4314 {
4315#if 0
4316 printf ("Compare2 %i\n", y * 6 + x);
4317 real_color.print (stdout);
4318 dufay_color.print (stdout);
4319#endif
4320 luminosity_t de = deltaE2000 (real_color, dufay_color, whitepoint);
4321 if (de > deltaEmax)
4322 deltaEmax = de;
4323 deltaEsum += de;
4324 n++;
4325 }
4326 }
4327 if (!tiff.write_row ())
4328 {
4329 *error = "write error";
4330 free (buffer);
4331 return false;
4332 }
4333 }
4334 }
4335 free (buffer);
4336 printf ("Average deltaE 2000 %f, max %f\n", deltaEsum / n, deltaEmax);
4337 return true;
4338}
4339/* optimized_xyz_matrix returns the optimized color matrix.
4340 OBSERVING_SPEC is the observing spectrum to use. */
4341color_matrix
4342spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::optimized_xyz_matrix (spectrum_dyes_to_xyz *observing_spec, progress_info *progress)
4343{
4344 if (observing_spec == nullptr)
4345 observing_spec = this;
4346 const int n = sizeof (TLCI_2012_TCS) / sizeof (xspect);
4347 rgbdata colors[n];
4348 xyz targets[n];
4349 rgbdata res = film_rgb_response (nullptr);
4350 luminosity_t sum = res.red + res.green + res.blue;
4351 res.red = res.green = res.blue = sum / 3;
4352 rgbdata scale = {1 / res.red, 1 / res.green, 1 / res.blue};
4353 for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
4354 {
4355 xspect &xtile = TLCI_2012_TCS [i];
4356 spectrum tile;
4357 compute_spectrum (tile, xtile);
4358 colors[i] = film_rgb_response (tile) * scale;
4359 targets[i] = get_xyz_old_observer (observing_spec->backlight, tile);
4360 }
4361 color_matrix m1 = determine_color_matrix (colors, targets, nullptr, n, observing_spec->whitepoint_xyz (), 0, nullptr, nullptr, {1,1,1}, progress);
4362 m1.scale_channels (1/rscale, 1/gscale, 1/bscale);
4363
4364 return m1;
4365}
4366
4367/* process_transformation_matrix returns the transformation matrix between two processes.
4368 FROM is the source spectrum. */
4369color_matrix
4370spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::process_transformation_matrix (spectrum_dyes_to_xyz *from)
4371{
4372 const int n = sizeof (TLCI_2012_TCS) / sizeof (xspect);
4373 rgbdata colors[n];
4374 rgbdata targets[n];
4375 rgbdata res = film_rgb_response (nullptr);
4376 //luminosity_t sum = res.red + res.green + res.blue;
4377 //res.red = res.green = res.blue = sum / 3;
4378 rgbdata scale = {1 / res.red, 1 / res.green, 1 / res.blue};
4379
4380 rgbdata res2 = from->film_rgb_response (nullptr);
4381 //luminosity_t sum2 = res2.red + res2.green + res2.blue;
4382 //res2.red = res2.green = res2.blue = sum2 / 3;
4383 rgbdata scale2 = {1 / res2.red, 1 / res2.green, 1 / res2.blue};
4384
4385 for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
4386 {
4387 xspect &xtile = TLCI_2012_TCS [i];
4388 spectrum tile;
4389 compute_spectrum (tile, xtile);
4390 colors[i] = from->film_rgb_response (tile) * scale2;
4391 targets[i] = film_rgb_response (tile) * scale;
4392#if 0
4393 printf ("%i\n",i);
4394 colors[i].print (stdout);
4395 targets[i].print (stdout);
4396#endif
4397 //printf ("%f %f %f : %f %f %f\n", colors[i].red, colors[i].green, colors[i].blue, targets[i].red, targets[i].green, targets[i].blue);
4398 }
4399 color_matrix m1 = determine_color_matrix (colors, nullptr, targets, n, whitepoint_xyz ());
4400 m1.scale_channels (1/rscale, 1/gscale, 1/bscale);
4401
4402 return m1;
4403}
4404/* write_spectra writes spectra to given files.
4405 REDS, GREENS, BLUES are filenames for dyes.
4406 BACKLIGHTS is filename for backlight.
4407 START, END define the spectral range.
4408 ABSORBANCE if true, write absorbance instead of transmitance. */
4409void
4410spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::write_spectra (const char *reds, const char *greens, const char *blues, const char *backlights, int start, int end, bool absorbance)
4411{
4412 if (reds)
4413 {
4414 FILE *f = fopen (reds, "wt");
4415 if (absorbance)
4416 print_absorbance_spectrum (f, red, start, end);
4417 else
4418 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, red, start, end);
4419 fclose (f);
4420 }
4421 if (greens)
4422 {
4423 FILE *f = fopen (greens, "wt");
4424 if (absorbance)
4425 print_absorbance_spectrum (f, green, start, end);
4426 else
4427 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, green, start, end);
4428 fclose (f);
4429 }
4430 if (blues)
4431 {
4432 FILE *f = fopen (blues, "wt");
4433 if (absorbance)
4434 print_absorbance_spectrum (f, blue, start, end);
4435 else
4436 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, blue, start, end);
4437 fclose (f);
4438 }
4439 if (backlights)
4440 {
4441 FILE *f = fopen (backlights, "wt");
4442 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, backlight, start, end);
4443 fclose (f);
4444 }
4445}

4498static void
4499print_response_spectrum (FILE * out, const spectrum spec, int start = SPECTRUM_START, int end = SPECTRUM_END)
4500{
4501 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4502 if (i * SPECTRUM_STEP + SPECTRUM_START >= start && i * SPECTRUM_STEP + SPECTRUM_START <= end)
4503 {
4504 //if (spec[i])
4505 fprintf (out, "%i %f\n", i * SPECTRUM_STEP + SPECTRUM_START, log10 (spec[i]));
4506 //else
4507 //fprintf (out, "%i\n", i * SPECTRUM_STEP + SPECTRUM_START, log10 (spec[i]));
4508 //assert (spec[i] > -100 && spec [i] < 100);
4509 }
4510}

4512static void
4513print_transmitance_spectrum_json (FILE *f, const char *name, spectrum s)
4514{
4515 fprintf (f, " \"%s\": [ ", name);
4516 fprintf (f, " %f", s[0]);
4517 for (int i = 1; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4518 fprintf (f, ", %f", s[i]);
4519 fprintf (f, "]");
4520}

4522/* write_ssf_json writes spectral sensitivity function to JSON file.
4523 NAME is the file name to write to. */
4524void
4525spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::write_ssf_json (const char *name)
4526{
4527 FILE *f = fopen (name, "wt");
4528 fprintf (f, "// Simulated spectral sensitivity function generated by ColorScreen\n"
4529 "// Use by dcamprof software\n"
4530 "{\n"
4531 " \"camera_name\": \"simulated additive process\",\n"
4532 " \"ssf_bands\": [ %i, %i, %i ],\n", SPECTRUM_START, SPECTRUM_END, SPECTRUM_STEP);
4533 spectrum sred, sgreen, sblue;
4534 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4535 {
4536 sred[i] = red[i] * film_response[i];
4537 sgreen[i] = green[i] * film_response[i];
4538 sblue[i] = blue[i] * film_response[i];
4539 }
4540 print_transmitance_spectrum_json (f, "red_ssf", sred);
4541 fprintf (f, ",\n");
4542 print_transmitance_spectrum_json (f, "green_ssf", sgreen);
4543 fprintf (f, ",\n");
4544 print_transmitance_spectrum_json (f, "blue_ssf", sblue);
4545 fprintf (f, "\n}");
4546}

4548/* write_responses writes responses to given files.
4549 REDS, GREENS, BLUES are filenames for the responses.
4550 LOG if true, write log10 of response.
4551 START, END define the spectral range. */
4552void
4553spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::write_responses (const char *reds, const char *greens, const char *blues, bool log, int start, int end)
4554{
4555 spectrum sred, sgreen, sblue;
4556 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4557 {
4558 sred[i] = red[i] * film_response[i];
4559 sgreen[i] = green[i] * film_response[i];
4560 sblue[i] = blue[i] * film_response[i];
4561 }
4562 if (reds)
4563 {
4564 FILE *f = fopen (reds, "wt");
4565 if (log)
4566 print_response_spectrum (f, sred);
4567 else
4568 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, sred, start, end);
4569 fclose (f);
4570 }
4571 if (greens)
4572 {
4573 FILE *f = fopen (greens, "wt");
4574 if (log)
4575 print_response_spectrum (f, sgreen);
4576 else
4577 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, sgreen, start, end);
4578 fclose (f);
4579 }
4580 if (blues)
4581 {
4582 FILE *f = fopen (blues, "wt");
4583 if (log)
4584 print_response_spectrum (f, sblue, start, end);
4585 else
4586 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, sblue, start, end);
4587 fclose (f);
4588 }
4589}
4590/* write_film_response writes film response of the film covered by optional filter specified by FI.
4591 FILENAME is the file name to write to.
4592 ABSOLUTE if true, do not scale by backlight.
4593 LOG if true, write log10 of response. */
4594bool
4595spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::write_film_response (const char *filename, luminosity_t *fi, bool absolute, bool log)
4596{
4597 FILE *f = fopen (filename, "wt");
4598 if (!f)
4599 return false;
4600 luminosity_t sum = 0;
4601 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4602 sum += film_response[i] * (!absolute ? backlight[i] : 1);
4603 spectrum s;
4604 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4605 {
4606 s[i] = (film_response[i] * (!absolute ? backlight[i] : 1) * (fi ? fi[i] : 1) * 100) / sum;
4607 //printf ("%s %f %f %f %f\n", filename, s[i], film_response[i] , (!absolute ? backlight[i] : 1), (fi ? fi[i] : 1));
4608 }
4609 if (log)
4610 print_response_spectrum (f, s);
4611 else
4612 print_transmitance_spectrum (f, s);
4613 fclose (f);
4614 return true;
4615}

4617/* Output tiff file showing primaries. */
4618bool
4619tiff_with_strips (const char *filename, xyz filter_red, xyz filter_green, xyz filter_blue, xyz background, xyz white)
4620{
4621 void *buffer;
4622 size_t len = create_pro_photo_rgb_profile (&buffer, srgb_white);
4623 tiff_writer_params par;
4624 par.filename=filename;
4625 par.width = par.height = 19;
4626 par.height = 19;
4627 par.height = 19;
4628 par.depth = 32;
4629 par.hdr = true;
4630 par.icc_profile = buffer;
4631 par.icc_profile_len = len;
4632 const char *error;
4633 tiff_writer tiff (par, &error);
4634 if (error)
4635 {
4636 free (buffer);
4637 return false;
4638 }
4639 luminosity_t r,g,b;
4640 for (int i = 0; i < 19; i++)
4641 {
4642 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (background.x, background.y, background.z, &r, &g, &b);
4643 for (int j = 0; j < 19; j++)
4644 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (j, r, g, b);
4645 //filter_red.print (stdout);
4646 //filter_green.print (stdout);
4647 //filter_blue.print (stdout);
4648 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (filter_red.x, filter_red.y, filter_red.z, &r, &g, &b);
4649 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (4, r, g, b);
4650 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (5, r, g, b);
4651 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (6, r, g, b);
4652 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (filter_green.x, filter_green.y, filter_green.z, &r, &g, &b);
4653 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (8, r, g, b);
4654 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (9, r, g, b);
4655 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (10, r, g, b);
4656 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (filter_blue.x, filter_blue.y, filter_blue.z, &r, &g, &b);
4657 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (12, r, g, b);
4658 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (13, r, g, b);
4659 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (14, r, g, b);
4660 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (white.x, white.y, white.z, &r, &g, &b);
4661 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (16, r, g, b);
4662 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (17, r, g, b);
4663 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (18, r, g, b);
4664 if (!tiff.write_row ())
4665 {
4666 free (buffer);
4667 return false;
4668 }
4669 }
4670 free (buffer);
4671 return true;
4672}
4673/* Output tiff file showing primaries. */
4674bool
4675spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::tiff_with_spectra_photo (const char *filename)
4676{
4677 tiff_writer_params par;
4678 par.filename=filename;
4679 const int w = 100;
4680 void *buffer;
4681 size_t len = create_pro_photo_rgb_profile (&buffer, srgb_white);
4682 par.width = SPECTRUM_END - SPECTRUM_START;
4683 par.height = w * 4;
4684 par.depth = 32;
4685 par.hdr = true;
4686 par.icc_profile = buffer;
4687 par.icc_profile_len = len;
4688 const char *error;
4689 tiff_writer tiff (par, &error);
4690 spectrum bandf;
4691 const int minp = 400;
4692 const int maxp = 780;
4693 const double powf = 1.5;
4694 if (error)
4695 return false;
4696 luminosity_t max = 0;
4697 for (int x = 0; x < SPECTRUM_END - SPECTRUM_START; x++)
4698 {
4699 int band = x + SPECTRUM_START;
4700 set_illuminant_to (bandf, il_band, band);
4701 luminosity_t l = simulated_response (backlight, film_response, bandf, nullptr);
4702 if (l > max)
4703 max = l;
4704 }
4705 for (int y = 0; y < w * 4; y++)
4706 {
4707 for (int x = 0; x < par.width; x++)
4708 {
4709 luminosity_t lxp = pow ((x-1) / (luminosity_t)par.width, powf);
4710 luminosity_t xp = pow (x / (luminosity_t)par.width, powf);
4711 luminosity_t nxp = pow ((x+1) / (luminosity_t)par.width, powf);
4712 int lband = minp + lxp * (maxp - minp);
4713 int band = minp + xp * (maxp - minp);
4714 int nband = minp + nxp * (maxp - minp);
4715 if ((((int)lband) / 100) < ((int)nband) / 100 && (y % w) > 3 *w /4)
4716 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (x, 1, 0, 0);
4717 else if ((((int)lband) / 50) < ((int)nband) / 50 && (y % w) > 5 *w /6)
4718 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (x, 1, 0, 0);
4719#if 0
4720 else if (((band + 1) % 50) < 3 && (y % w) > 5 *w /6)
4721#endif
4722 else if ((((int)band) / 10) < ((int)nband) / 10 && (y % w) > 7 *w /8)
4723 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (x, 0, 0, 1);
4724 else
4725 {
4726 set_illuminant_to (bandf, il_band, band);
4727 luminosity_t *s = y < w ? blue : y < 2 * w ? green : y < 3 * w ? red : nullptr, r, g, b;
4728 if ((y % w) < 2 * w / 3)
4729 {
4730 luminosity_t l = simulated_response (backlight, film_response, bandf, s);
4731 l /= max;
4732 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (l, l, l, &r, &g, &b);
4733 }
4734 else
4735 {
4736 xyz c;
4737 if (s)
4738 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4739 bandf[i] *= s[i];
4740 c = get_xyz_old_observer (backlight, bandf);
4741 c = c * 15;
4742 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (c.x, c.y, c.z, &r, &g, &b);
4743 }
4744 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (x, r, g, b);
4745 }
4746#if 0
4747 else
4748 {
4749 set_backlight (il_band, band);
4750 xyz color = whitepoint_xyz ();
4751 luminosity_t r,g,b;
4752 color.to_srgb (&r, &g, &b);
4753 r = std::min (std::max (r, (luminosity_t)0), (luminosity_t)1);
4754 g = std::min (std::max (g, (luminosity_t)0), (luminosity_t)1);
4755 b = std::min (std::max (b, (luminosity_t)0), (luminosity_t)1);
4756 tiff.put_pixel (x, r* 65535, g * 65535, b * 65535);
4757 }
4758#endif
4759 }
4760 if (!tiff.write_row ())
4761 return false;
4762 }
4763 return true;
4764}

4777bool
4778spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::tiff_with_overlapping_filters (const char *filename, rgbdata white, const char *spectra_prefix)
4779{
4780 xyz filter_white = dyes_rgb_to_xyz (white.red, white.green, white.blue);
4781 xyz whitepoint = whitepoint_xyz ();
4782 void *buffer;
4783 size_t len = create_pro_photo_rgb_profile (&buffer, whitepoint);
4784
4785 tiff_writer_params par;
4786 par.filename=filename;
4787 par.width = 19;
4788 par.height = 19;
4789 par.depth = 32;
4790 par.hdr = true;
4791 par.icc_profile = buffer;
4792 par.icc_profile_len = len;
4793 const char *error;
4794 tiff_writer tiff (par, &error);
4795 if (error)
4796 {
4797 free (buffer);
4798 return false;
4799 }
4800 luminosity_t r,g,b;
4801 for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
4802 {
4803 int br = /*200*/1;
4804 for (int d = 0; d < (i == 4 ? 3 : 4); d++)
4805 {
4806 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (filter_white.x * br, filter_white.y * br, filter_white.z * br, &r, &g, &b);
4807 int i1 = i == 0 || i == 4 || d == 3 ? -1 : i-1;
4808 for (int j = 0; j < 19; j++)
4809 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (j, r, g, b);
4810 for (int j = 0; j < 5; j++)
4811 {
4812 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (filter_white.x * br, filter_white.y * br, filter_white.z * br, &r, &g, &b);
4813 for (int d2 = 0; d2 < (j == 4 ? 3 : 4); d2++)
4814 {
4815 int mul = 1;
4816 const char *filename = NULL;
4817 const char *filename2 = NULL;
4818 std::string sfile, sfile2;
4819 int i2 = j == 0 || j == 4 || d2 == 3 ? -1 : j-1;
4820 if (spectra_prefix)
4821 {
4822 const char *name[3]={"red","green","blue"};
4823 sfile = std::string (spectra_prefix);
4824 sfile2 = std::string (spectra_prefix);
4825 if (i1 >= 0)
4826 {
4827 sfile = sfile + std::string (name[i1]);
4828 sfile2 = sfile2 + std::string (name[i1]);
4829 }
4830 if (i2 >= 0)
4831 {
4832 sfile = sfile + std::string (name[i2]);
4833 sfile2 = sfile2 + std::string (name[i2]);
4834 }
4835 sfile = sfile + ".dat";
4836 sfile2 = sfile2 + "-backlight.dat";
4837 filename = sfile.c_str ();
4838 filename2 = sfile2.c_str ();
4839 }
4840 xyz f = combined_xyz (i1 < 0 ? nullptr : i1 == 0 ? red : i1 == 1 ? green : blue,
4841 i2 < 0 ? nullptr : i2 == 0 ? red : i2 == 1 ? green : blue,
4842 backlight, filename, filename2);
4843 int br2 = (i && j) ? br : 1;
4844 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (f.x * br2, f.y * br2, f.z * br2, &r, &g, &b);
4845 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (j*4+d2, r*mul, g*mul, b*mul);
4846 }
4847 }
4848 if (!tiff.write_row ())
4849 {
4850 free (buffer);
4851 return false;
4852 }
4853 }
4854 }
4855 free (buffer);
4856 return true;
4857}
4858bool
4859spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::tiff_with_overlapping_filters_response (const char *filename, rgbdata white)
4860{
4861 tiff_writer_params par;
4862 par.filename=filename;
4863 par.width = 23;
4864 par.height = 19;
4865 par.depth = 32;
4866 par.hdr = true;
4867 void *buffer;
4868 size_t len = create_pro_photo_rgb_profile (&buffer, whitepoint_xyz ());
4869 par.icc_profile = buffer;
4870 par.icc_profile_len = len;
4871 const char *error;
4872 tiff_writer tiff (par, &error);
4873 if (error)
4874 {
4875 free (buffer);
4876 return false;
4877 }
4878 luminosity_t gamma = 1.8;
4879
4880 rgbdata dred = film_rgb_response (red);
4881 rgbdata dgreen = film_rgb_response (green);
4882 rgbdata dblue = film_rgb_response (blue);
4883 rgbdata dwhite = film_rgb_response (nullptr);
4884 /*TODO apply sensitivity curve. */
4885 luminosity_t dglass = simulated_response (backlight, film_response);
4886 color_matrix m = xyz_matrix ();
4887
4888 for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
4889 {
4890 for (int d = 0; d < (i == 4 ? 3 : 4); d++)
4891 {
4892 int i1 = i == 0 || i == 4 || d == 3 ? -1 : i-1;
4893 luminosity_t g = invert_gamma (dglass, gamma);
4894 for (int j = 0; j < 19; j++)
4895 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (j, g, g, g);
4896 for (int j = 0; j < 5; j++)
4897 {
4898 for (int d2 = 0; d2 < (j == 4 ? 3 : 4); d2++)
4899 {
4900 luminosity_t dd;
4901
4902 int i2 = j == 0 || j == 4 || d2 == 3 ? -1 : j-1;
4903 if (i1 == -1 && i2 == -1)
4904 dd = dglass;
4905 else if (i1 == -1 || i2 == -1)
4906 {
4907 int c = i1 == -1 ? i2 : i1;
4908 dd = dwhite[c];
4909 }
4910 else if (i1 == 0)
4911 dd = dred[i2];
4912 else if (i1 == 1)
4913 dd = dgreen[i2];
4914 else
4915 dd = dblue[i2];
4916 g = invert_gamma (dd, gamma);
4917 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (j*4+d2, g,g,g);
4918 }
4919 }
4920 luminosity_t r=1, b=1;
4921 g=1;
4922 if (i1 != -1 || (i == 0 && d < 3))
4923 {
4924 rgbdata &f = i == 0 ? dwhite : (i1 == 0) ? dred : (i1 == 1) ? dgreen : dblue;
4925 //xyz c = (filter_red * f.red) + (filter_green * f.green) + (filter_blue * f.blue);
4926 xyz c (0,0,0);
4927 m.apply_to_rgb (f.red * white.red, f.green * white.green, f.blue * white.blue, &c.x, &c.y, &c.z);
4928 xyz_to_pro_photo_rgb (c.x, c.y, c.z, &r, &g, &b);
4929 }
4930 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (19, 0, 0, 0);
4931 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (20, r, g, b);
4932 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (21, r, g, b);
4933 tiff.put_hdr_pixel (22, r, g, b);
4934 if (!tiff.write_row ())
4935 return false;
4936 }
4937 }
4938 free (buffer);
4939 return true;
4940}
4941
4942bool
4943write_optimal_response (color_matrix m, const char *redname, const char *greenname, const char *bluename, luminosity_t rw, luminosity_t gw, luminosity_t bw)
4944{
4945 FILE *redf = fopen (redname, "wt");
4946 if (!redf)
4947 return false;
4948 FILE *greenf = fopen (greenname, "wt");
4949 if (!greenf)
4950 {
4951 fclose (redf);
4952 return false;
4953 }
4954 FILE *bluef = fopen (bluename, "wt");
4955 if (!bluef)
4956 {
4957 fclose (redf);
4958 fclose (greenf);
4959 return false;
4960 }
4961 color_matrix im = m.invert ();
4962 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
4963 {
4964 luminosity_t r, g, b;
4965 int freq = i * SPECTRUM_STEP + SPECTRUM_START;
4966 im.apply_to_rgb (cie_cmf_x[i], cie_cmf_y[i], cie_cmf_z[i], &r, &g, &b);
4967 fprintf (redf, "%f %f\n", (double)freq, r/rw);
4968 fprintf (greenf, "%f %f\n", (double)freq, g/gw);
4969 fprintf (bluef, "%f %f\n", (double)freq, b/bw);
4970 }
4971 fclose (redf);
4972 fclose (greenf);
4973 fclose (bluef);
4974 return true;
4975}

4995/* SET_DYES sets the dyes spectra to given measured spectra.
4996 DYES is the measured spectra to use.
4997 If DYES2 is set and AGE > 0, then mix the two spectras in given ratio. */
4998void
4999spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::set_dyes (enum dyes dyes, enum dyes dyes2, rgbdata age)
5000{
5001#if 0
5002 if ((int)dyes >= (int)dufaycolor_color_cinematography
5003 && (int)dyes <= (int)dufaycolor_narrow_cut_filters_harrison_horner)
5004 {
5005 rscale = dufaycolor::red_size;
5006 gscale = dufaycolor::green_size;
5007 bscale = dufaycolor::blue_size;
5008 }
5009#endif
5010 subtractive = set_dyes_to (red, green, blue, cyan, magenta, yellow, dyes);
5011 if ((age.red != 0 || age.green != 0 || age.blue != 0) && dyes2 != dyes_max)
5012 {
5013 spectrum aged_red, aged_green, aged_blue;
5014 set_dyes_to (aged_red, aged_green, aged_blue, cyan, magenta, yellow, dyes2);
5015 for (int i = 0; i < SPECTRUM_SIZE; i++)
5016 {
5017 red[i] = red[i] * (1 - age.red) + aged_red[i] * age.red;
5018 green[i] = green[i] * (1 - age.green) + aged_green[i] * age.green;
5019 blue[i] = blue[i] * (1 - age.blue) + aged_blue[i] * age.blue;
5020 }
5021 }
5022}

5024/* SET_CHARACTERISTIC_CURVE sets the characteristic curve of the film.
5025 CURVE is the characteristic curve to use. */
5026void
5027spectrum_dyes_to_xyz::set_characteristic_curve (enum characteristic_curves curve)
5028{
5029 assert (!red_characteristic_curve);
5030 switch (curve)
5031 {
5032 case linear_reversal_curve:
5033 break;
5034 case input_curve:
5035 hd_curve_inst = std::make_unique<richards_hd_curve> (100, input_curve_params);
5036 red_characteristic_curve = green_characteristic_curve = blue_characteristic_curve = std::make_shared<film_sensitivity> (hd_curve_inst.get ());
5037 red_characteristic_curve->precompute ();
5038 break;
5039 case safe_output_curve:
5040 hd_curve_inst = std::make_unique<richards_hd_curve> (100, safe_output_curve_params);
5041 red_characteristic_curve = green_characteristic_curve = blue_characteristic_curve = std::make_shared<film_sensitivity> (hd_curve_inst.get ());
5042 red_characteristic_curve->precompute ();
5043 break;
5044 case safe_reversal_output_curve:
5045 hd_curve_inst = std::make_unique<richards_hd_curve> (100, safe_reversal_output_curve_params);
5046 red_characteristic_curve = green_characteristic_curve = blue_characteristic_curve = std::make_shared<film_sensitivity> (hd_curve_inst.get ());
5047 red_characteristic_curve->precompute ();
5048 break;
5049 case kodachrome25_curve:
5050 red_characteristic_curve = std::make_shared<film_sensitivity> (&film_sensitivity::kodachrome_25_red, 0, 4);
5051 red_characteristic_curve->precompute ();
5052 green_characteristic_curve = std::make_shared<film_sensitivity> (&film_sensitivity::kodachrome_25_green, 0, 4);
5053 green_characteristic_curve->precompute ();
5054 blue_characteristic_curve = std::make_shared<film_sensitivity> (&film_sensitivity::kodachrome_25_blue, 0, 4);
5055 blue_characteristic_curve->precompute ();
5056 break;
5057 /* Based on chart in The Spicer-Dudfay Colour Film Process, Thorne Baker, The Photograhic Jounral, March 1932, 109--117 */
5058 case spicer_dufay_curve_low:
5059 hd_curve_inst = std::make_unique<richards_hd_curve> (100, hd_curve_parameters(0.005596021177603383, 0.13326648483876236,
5060 0.9264367078453395, 0.25357372051981475,
5061 3.8351612385689076, 1.7539186587518052,
5062 3.8351612385689076, 1.7539186587518052));
5063
5064 red_characteristic_curve = green_characteristic_curve = blue_characteristic_curve = std::make_shared<film_sensitivity> (hd_curve_inst.get (), 0.25, 6);
5065 blue_characteristic_curve->precompute ();
5066 break;
5067 case spicer_dufay_curve_mid:
5068 hd_curve_inst = std::make_unique<richards_hd_curve> (100, hd_curve_parameters(0.005596021177603383, 0.13326648483876236,
5069 0.7346061286699825, 0.3354524306112632,
5070 2.7042515642547724, 2.207183539226697,
5071 2.7042515642547724, 2.207183539226697));
5072 red_characteristic_curve = green_characteristic_curve = blue_characteristic_curve = std::make_shared<film_sensitivity> (hd_curve_inst.get (), 0.25, 4);
5073 blue_characteristic_curve->precompute ();
5074 break;
5075 case spicer_dufay_curve_high:
5076 hd_curve_inst = std::make_unique<richards_hd_curve> (100, hd_curve_parameters(0.005596021177603383, 0.13326648483876236,
5077 0.664193807155463, 0.430406706240976,
5078 1.5716733515161239, 2.2480065778918665,
5079 1.5716733515161239, 2.2480065778918665));
5080 red_characteristic_curve = green_characteristic_curve = blue_characteristic_curve = std::make_shared<film_sensitivity> (hd_curve_inst.get (), 0.25, 4);
5081 blue_characteristic_curve->precompute ();
5082 break;
5083 case spicer_dufay_reversal_curve_low:
5084 hd_curve_inst = std::make_unique<richards_hd_curve> (100, hd_curve_parameters(
5085 0.10817262955238283, 1.7389218674795455,
5086 0.10817262955238283, 1.7389218674795455,
5087 3.1461832183539227, 0.19716428686026033,
5088 3.990309642226858, 0.10466468795122763));
5089
5090 red_characteristic_curve = green_characteristic_curve = blue_characteristic_curve = std::make_shared<film_sensitivity> (hd_curve_inst.get (), 0, 3000 /*6000*/);
5091 blue_characteristic_curve->precompute ();
5092 break;
5093 case spicer_dufay_reversal_curve_mid:
5094 hd_curve_inst = std::make_unique<richards_hd_curve> (100, hd_curve_parameters(
5095 1.2834525910476495, 2.253437349590888,
5096 1.2834525910476495, 2.253437349590888,
5097 3.262801219316541, 0.27367639980747693,
5098 3.990309642226858, 0.10466468795122763));
5099 red_characteristic_curve = green_characteristic_curve = blue_characteristic_curve = std::make_shared<film_sensitivity> (hd_curve_inst.get (),0 , 3000 /*20000*/);
5100 blue_characteristic_curve->precompute ();
5101 break;
5102 case spicer_dufay_reversal_curve_high:
5103 hd_curve_inst = std::make_unique<richards_hd_curve> (100, hd_curve_parameters(
5104 2.446334028557678, 2.2031926841007543,
5105 2.446334028557678, 2.2031926841007543,
5106 3.409735279961495, 0.34393951548211144,
5107 3.9904123215145204, 0.13004572437028772));
5108 red_characteristic_curve = green_characteristic_curve = blue_characteristic_curve = std::make_shared<film_sensitivity> (hd_curve_inst.get (),0 , 3000 /*30000*/);
5109 blue_characteristic_curve->precompute ();
5110 break;
5111 default:
